hds-font-weight-medium"><div class="version-switcher_versionDropdownItem__0MlWP">v1.7.x<!-- --> </div></span></a></li><li class="list-item_root__ZdUej"><a class="list-item_link__l6lDd g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow" href="/terraform/language/v1.6.x/style" rel="nofollow"><span class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-200 hds-font-weight-medium"><div class="version-switcher_versionDropdownItem__0MlWP">v1.6.x<!-- --> </div></span></a></li><li class="list-item_root__ZdUej"><a class="list-item_link__l6lDd g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow" href="/terraform/language/v1.5.x/style" rel="nofollow"><span class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-200 hds-font-weight-medium"><div class="version-switcher_versionDropdownItem__0MlWP">v1.5.x<!-- --> </div></span></a></li><li class="list-item_root__ZdUej"><a class="list-item_link__l6lDd g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow" href="/terraform/language/v1.4.x/style" rel="nofollow"><span class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-200 hds-font-weight-medium"><div class="version-switcher_versionDropdownItem__0MlWP">v1.4.x<!-- --> </div></span></a></li><li class="list-item_root__ZdUej"><a class="list-item_link__l6lDd g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow" href="/terraform/language/v1.3.x/style" rel="nofollow"><span class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-200 hds-font-weight-medium"><div class="version-switcher_versionDropdownItem__0MlWP">v1.3.x<!-- --> </div></span></a></li><li class="list-item_root__ZdUej"><a class="list-item_link__l6lDd g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow" href="/terraform/language/v1.2.x/style" rel="nofollow"><span class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-200 hds-font-weight-medium"><div class="version-switcher_versionDropdownItem__0MlWP">v1.2.x<!-- --> </div></span></a></li><li class="list-item_root__ZdUej"><a class="list-item_link__l6lDd g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow" href="/terraform/language/v1.1.x/style" rel="nofollow"><span class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-200 hds-font-weight-medium"><div class="version-switcher_versionDropdownItem__0MlWP">v1.1.x<!-- --> </div></span></a></li></ul></div></div></nav></div><div class="docs-page-heading_pageHeadingWrapper__LEMQ7"><h1 class="mds-typography-display-expressive-400 mds-typography-font-weight-bold docs-plain-page-heading_root__HzRuX g-offset-scroll-margin-top" id="style-guide">Style Guide</h1></div></div><div class="dev-dot-content_root__UJDct hds-typography-body-300"><div class="mdx-content-container"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The flexibility of Terraform&#x27;s configuration language gives you many options to choose from as you write your code, structure your directories, and test your configuration. While some design decisions depend on your organization&#x27;s needs or preferences, there are some common patterns that we suggest you adopt. Adopting and adhering to a style guide keeps your Terraform code legible, scalable, and maintainable.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">This article discusses best practices and some considerations to keep in mind as you develop your organization&#x27;s style guide. The article is split into two sections. The first section covers code style recommendations, such as formatting and resource organization. The second section covers operations and workflow recommendations, such as lifecycle management through meta-arguments, versioning, and sensitive data management.</p><h2 id="code-style" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="code style permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#code-style"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Code style</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Writing Terraform code in a consistent style makes it easier to read and maintain. The following sections discuss code style recommendations, including the following:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Run <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform fmt</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform validate</code> before committing your code to version control.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Use a linter such as <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://github.com/terraform-linters/tflint">TFLint</a> to enforce your organization&#x27;s own coding best practices.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Use <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">#</code> for single and multi-line comments.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Use nouns for resource names and do not include the resource type in the name.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Use underscores to separate multiple words in names. Wrap the resource type and name in double quotes in your resource definition.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Let your code build on itself: define dependent resources after the resources they reference.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Include a type and description for every variable.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Include a description for every output.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Avoid overuse of variables and local values.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Always include a default provider configuration.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Use <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">count</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">for_each</code> sparingly.</li></ul><h2 id="code-formatting" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="code formatting permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#code-formatting"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Code formatting</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The Terraform parser allows you some flexibility in how you lay out the elements in your configuration files, but the Terraform language also has some idiomatic style conventions which we recommend users always follow for consistency between files and modules written by different teams.</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Indent two spaces for each nesting level</p></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">When multiple arguments with single-line values appear on consecutive lines at the same nesting level, align their equals signs:</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_1424bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">ami           </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;abc123&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">instance_type </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;t2.micro&quot;</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">When both arguments and blocks appear together inside a block body, place all of the arguments together at the top and then place nested blocks below them. Use one blank line to separate the arguments from the blocks.</p></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Use empty lines to separate logical groups of arguments within a block.</p></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For blocks that contain both arguments and &quot;meta-arguments&quot; (as defined by the Terraform language semantics), list meta-arguments first and separate them from other arguments with one blank line. Place meta-argument blocks last and separate them from other blocks with one blank line. Refer to <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/style#dynamic-resource-count">dynamic resource count</a> for more information on meta-arguments.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_1a24bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">resource</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws_instance&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;example&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">  # meta-argument first</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  count </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> 2</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  ami           </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;abc123&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  instance_type </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;t2.micro&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">  network_interface</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">    # ...</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  }</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">  # meta-argument block last</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">  lifecycle</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">    create_before_destroy </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> true</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  }</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Top-level blocks should always be separated from one another by one blank line. Nested blocks should also be separated by blank lines, except when grouping together related blocks of the same type (like multiple <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">provisioner</code> blocks in a resource).</p></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Avoid grouping multiple blocks of the same type with other blocks of a different type, unless the block types are defined by semantics to form a family. (For example: <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">root_block_device</code>, <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">ebs_block_device</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">ephemeral_block_device</code> on <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_instance</code> form a family of block types describing AWS block devices, and can therefore be grouped together and mixed.)</p></li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform fmt</code> command formats your Terraform configuration to a subset of the above recommendations. By default, the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform fmt</code> command will only modify your Terraform code in the directory that you execute it in, but you can include the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">-recursive</code> flag to modify code in all subdirectories as well.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend that you run <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform fmt</code> before each commit to version control. You can use mechanisms such as <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://git-scm.com/book/en/v2/Customizing-Git-Git-Hooks">Git pre-commit hooks</a> to automatically run this command each time you commit your code.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you use Microsoft VS Code, use the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=HashiCorp.terraform">Terraform VS Code extension</a> to enable features such as syntax highlighting and validation, automatic code formatting, and integration with HCP Terraform. If your development environment or text editor supports the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://microsoft.github.io/language-server-protocol/">Language Server Protocol</a>, you can use the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://github.com/hashicorp/terraform-ls">Terraform Language Server</a> to access most of the VS Code extension features.</p><h2 id="code-validation" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="code validation permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#code-validation"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Code validation</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform validate</code> command checks that your configuration is syntactically valid and internally consistent. The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">validate</code> command does not check if argument values are valid for a specific provider, but it will verify that they are the correct type. It does not evaluate any existing state.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform validate</code> command is safe to run automatically and frequently. You can configure your text editor to run this command as a post-save check, define it as a pre-commit hook in a Git repository, or run it as a step in a CI/CD pipeline.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For more information, refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/cli/commands/validate">Terraform <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">validate</code> documentation</a>.</p><h2 id="file-names" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="file names permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#file-names"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>File names</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend the following file naming conventions:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">backend.tf</code> file that contains your <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/backend">backend configuration</a>. You can define multiple <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform</code> blocks in your configuration to separate your backend configuration from your Terraform and provider versioning configuration.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">main.tf</code> file that contains all resource and data source blocks.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">outputs.tf</code> file that contains all output blocks in alphabetical order.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">providers.tf</code> file that contains all <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">provider</code> blocks and configuration.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform.tf</code> file that contains a single <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform</code> block which defines your <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">required_version</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">required_providers</code>.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">variables.tf</code> file that contains all variable blocks in alphabetical order.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">locals.tf</code> file that contains local values. Refer to <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/style#local-values">local values</a> for more information.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">override.tf</code> file that contains override definitions for your configuration. Terraform loads this and all files ending with <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">_override.tf</code> last. Use them sparingly and add comments to the original resource definitions, as these overrides make your code harder to reason about. Refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/files/override">override files</a> documentation for more information.</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">As your codebase grows, limiting it to just these files can become difficult to maintain. If your code becomes hard to navigate due to its size, we recommend that you organize resources and data sources in separate files by logical groups. For example, if your web application requires networking, storage, and compute resources, you might create the following files:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">network.tf</code> file that contains your VPC, subnets, load balancers, and all other networking resources.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">storage.tf</code> file that contains your object storage and related permissions configuration.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">compute.tf</code> file that contains your compute instances.</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">No matter how you decide to split your code, it should be immediately clear where a maintainer can find a specific resource or data source definition.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">As your configuration grows, you may need to separate it into multiple state files. The HashiCorp Well-Architected Framework provides more guidance about <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/well-architected-framework/operational-excellence/operational-excellence-workspaces-projects#workspace-structure">configuration structure and scope</a>.</p><h2 id="linting-and-static-code-analysis" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="linting and static code analysis permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#linting-and-static-code-analysis"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Linting and static code analysis</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Terraform does not have a built-in linter, but many organizations rely on a third-party linting tool such as <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://github.com/terraform-linters/tflint">TFLint</a> to enforce code standards. A linter uses static code analysis to compare your Terraform code against a set of rules. Most linters ship with a default set of rules, but also let you write your own.</p><h2 id="comments" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="comments permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#comments"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Comments</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Write your code so it is easy to understand. Only when necessary, use comments to clarify complexity for other maintainers.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Use <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">#</code> for both single- and multi-line comments. The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">//</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">/* */</code> comment syntaxes are not considered idiomatic, but Terraform supports them to remain backward-compatible with earlier versions of HCL.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_74bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)"># Each tunnel is responsible for encrypting and decrypting traffic exiting</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)"># and leaving its associated gateway.</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">resource</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;google_compute_vpn_tunnel&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;tunnel1&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">  ## ...</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><h2 id="resource-naming" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="resource naming permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#resource-naming"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Resource naming</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Every resource within a configuration must have a unique name. For consistency and readability, use a descriptive noun and separate words with underscores. Do not include the resource type in the resource identifier since the resource address already includes it. Wrap the resource type and name in double quotes.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">❌ Bad:</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_84bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">resource</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> aws_instance</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> webAPI-aws-instance</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">...</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">✅ Good:</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_8kbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">resource</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws_instance&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;web_api&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">...</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><h2 id="resource-order" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="resource order permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#resource-order"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Resource order</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The order of the resources and data sources in your code does not affect how Terraform builds them, so organize your resources for readability. Terraform determines the creation order based on cross-resource dependencies.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">How you order your resources largely depends on the size and complexity of your code, but we recommend defining data sources alongside the resources that reference them. For readability, your Terraform code should “build on itself” — you should define a data source before the resource that references it.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The following example defines an <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_instance</code> that relies on two data sources, <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_ami</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_availability_zone</code>. For readability and continuity, it defines the data sources before the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_instance</code> resource.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_9sbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">data</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws_ami&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;web&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">  ##...</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">data</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws_availability_zones&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;available&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">  ##...</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">resource</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws_instance&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;web&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  ami               </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> data.aws_ami.web.id</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  availability_zone </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> data.aws_availability_zones.available.names[</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)">0</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">]</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">  ##...</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend following a consistent order for resource parameters:</p><ol class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">If present, The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">count</code> or <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">for_each</code> meta-argument.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Resource-specific non-block parameters.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Resource-specific block parameters.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">If required, a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">lifecycle</code> block.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">If required, the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">depends_on</code> parameter.</li></ol><h2 id="variables" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="variables permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#variables"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Variables</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">While variables make your modules more flexible, overusing variables can make code difficult to understand. When deciding whether to expose a variable for a resource setting, consider whether that parameter will change between deployments.</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Define a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">type</code> and a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">description</code> for every variable.</p></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If the variable is optional, define a reasonable <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">default</code>.</p></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For sensitive variables, such as passwords and private keys, set the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">sensitive</code> parameter to <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">true</code>. Remember that Terraform will still store this value in plain text in its state, but it will not display it when you run <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform plan</code> or <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform apply</code>. Refer to <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/style#secrets-management">secrets management</a> for more information on how to securely handle sensitive values.</p></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Use <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/block/variable#validation">input variable validation</a> to create additional rules for your variable values in addition to Terraform&#x27;s type validation. Only use variable validation when your variable values have uniquely restrictive requirements. For example, if your Terraform configuration requires two web instances, add a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">validation</code> block to enforce it:</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_18b4bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">variable</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;web_instance_count&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  type        </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)"> number</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  description </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;Number of web instances to deploy. This application requires at least two instances.&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">

</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">    condition     </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> var.web_instance_count </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">&gt;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> 1</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">    error_message </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;This application requires at least two web instances.&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  }</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div></li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend following a consistent order for variable parameters:</p><ol class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Type</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Description</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Default (optional)</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Sensitive (optional)</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/block/variable#validation">Validation blocks</a></li></ol><h2 id="outputs" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="outputs permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#outputs"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Outputs</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Output values let you expose data about your infrastructure on the command line and make it easy to reference in other Terraform configurations. Like you would for variables, provide a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">type</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">description</code> for each output.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend that you use the following order for your output parameters:</p><ol class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Type</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Description</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Value</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Sensitive (optional)</li></ol><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Every variable and output requires a unique name. For consistency and readability, we recommend that you use a descriptive noun and separate words with underscores.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_d4bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">variable</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;db_disk_size&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>

</span></span></code></pre></div></div><h2 id="local-values" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="local values permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#local-values"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Local values</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Local values let you reference an <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/expressions">expression</a> or value multiple times. Use local values sparingly, as overuse can make your code harder to understand.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For example, you can use a local value to create a suffix for the region and environment (for example, <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">development</code> or <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">test</code>), and append it to multiple resources.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_e4bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">locals</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  name_suffix </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">${</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">var</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">.</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">region</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">}</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">-</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">${</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">var</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">.</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">environment</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">}</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">resource</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws_instance&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;web&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  ami           </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> data.aws_ami.ubuntu.id</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  instance_type </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;t3.micro&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  tags </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">    Name </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;web-</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">${</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">local</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">.</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">name_suffix</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">}</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  }</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Define local values in one of two places:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">If you reference the local value in multiple files, define it in a file named <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">locals.tf</code>.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">If the local value is specific to a file, define it at the top of that file.</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">As for other Terraform objects, use descriptive nouns for local value names and underscores to separate multiple words.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For more information, refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">locals</code> block</a> and the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/tutorials/configuration-language/locals">Simplify Terraform configuration with locals</a> tutorial.</p><h2 id="provider-aliasing" mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Provider aliasing lets you define multiple <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">provider</code> blocks for the same Terraform provider. Potential use cases for aliases include provisioning resources in multiple regions within a single configuration. The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">provider</code> meta-argument for resources and the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">providers</code> meta-argument for modules specifies which provider to use.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"><p class="mds-typography-legacy-body-200 mds-typography-font-weight-semibold title__nX-Ny">providers.tf</p></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_fsbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">provider</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>

</span></span></code></pre></div></div><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Any provider block that does not define the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">alias</code> parameter is the default provider configuration.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Always include a default provider configuration and define all of your providers in the same file.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">If you define multiple instances of a provider, define the default first.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">For non-default providers, define the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy hds-typography-code-200">provider</code> block.</li></ul><h2 id="dynamic-resource-count" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="dynamic resource count permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#dynamic-resource-count"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Dynamic resource count</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">for_each</code> and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">count</code> meta-arguments let you create multiple resources from a single <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">resource</code> block depending on run-time conditions. You can use these meta-arguments to make your code flexible and reduce duplicate resource blocks. If the resources are almost identical, use <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">count</code>. If some of the arguments need distinct values that you cannot derive from an integer, use <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">for_each</code>.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">for_each</code> meta-argument accepts a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">map</code> or <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">set</code> value, and Terraform will create an instance of that resource for each element in the value you provide. In the following example, Terraform creates an <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_instance</code> for each of the strings defined in the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">web_instances</code> variable: &quot;ui&quot;, &quot;api&quot;, &quot;db&quot; and &quot;metrics&quot;. The example uses <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">each.key</code> to give each instance a unique name. The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">web_private_ips</code> output uses a <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/expressions/for">for expression</a> to create a map of instance names and their private IP addresses, while the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">web_ui_public_ip</code> output addresses the instance with the key &quot;ui&quot; directly.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_hcbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">variable</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;web_instances&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>

</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The above example will create the following output:</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_hsbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">web_private_ips </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">  &quot;api&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)"> =</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;172.31.25.29&quot;</span></span>

</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)">  &quot;metrics&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)"> =</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;172.31.26.112&quot;</span></span>

</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>

</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/meta-arguments#for_each">for_each meta-argument documentation</a> for more examples.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">count</code> meta-argument lets you create multiple instances of a resource from a single resource block. Refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/meta-arguments#count">count meta-argument documentation</a> for examples.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">A common practice to conditionally create resources is to use the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">count</code> meta-argument with a <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/expressions/conditionals">conditional expression</a>. In the following example, Terraform will only create the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_instance</code> if <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">var.enable_metrics</code> is <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">true</code>.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_isbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">variable</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;enable_metrics&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>

</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>

</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">resource</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;aws_instance&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;web&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight__a84m6 line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  count </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> var.enable_metrics </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">?</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> 1</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)"> :</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> 0</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
