</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Meta-arguments simplify your code but add complexity, so use them in moderation. If the effect of the meta-argument is not immediately obvious, use a comment for clarification.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">To learn more about these meta-arguments, refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/meta-arguments#for_each"><code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">for_each</code></a> and <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/meta-arguments#count"><code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">count</code></a> documentation.</p><h2 id="gitignore" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="gitignore permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#gitignore"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>.gitignore</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Define a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">.gitignore</code> file for your repository to exclude files that you should not publish to version control, such as your state file.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Do not commit:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Your <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform.tfstate</code> state file, including <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform.tfstate.*</code> backup
state files. The state file can contain secrets and other sensitive
information. Also, version control systems do not support locking the state
file so that it can only be modified by one instance of Terraform at a time.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Your <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">.terraform.tfstate.lock.info</code> file. Terraform creates and deletes this
file automatically when you run a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform apply</code> command and contains info
about your <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/state/locking">state lock</a></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Your <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">.terraform</code> directory, where Terraform downloads providers and child modules. </li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/cli/commands/plan#out-filename">Saved plan files</a> that you create when you include the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">-out</code> flag when you run <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform plan</code>.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Any <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">.tfvars</code> files that contain sensitive information.</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Always commit:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">All Terraform code files</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Your <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">.terraform.lock.hcl</code> <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/files/dependency-lock">dependency lock file</a></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">.gitignore</code> file that excludes the files listed below</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">README.md</code> to describe the code, input variables, and outputs</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For an example, refer to <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://github.com/github/gitignore/blob/main/Terraform.gitignore">GitHub&#x27;s Terraform .gitignore file</a>.</p><h2 id="workflow-style" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="workflow style permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#workflow-style"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Workflow style</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">This section reviews standards that enable predictable and secure Terraform workflows, such as:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Pin your Terraform, provider, and module versions.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Name your module repositories using this three-part name <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform-&lt;PROVIDER&gt;-&lt;NAME&gt;</code> when using the HCP Terraform registry.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Store local modules at <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">./modules/&lt;module_name&gt;</code>.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Use the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://registry.terraform.io/providers/hashicorp/tfe/latest/docs/data-sources/outputs"><code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">tfe_outputs</code></a> data source or provider-specific data sources to share state between two state files.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Protect credentials by using <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/tutorials/cloud/dynamic-credentials">dynamic provider credentials</a> or a secrets manager such as HashiCorp Vault.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Write <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/tests">tests</a> for your modules.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Use <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/cloud-docs/policy-enforcement">policy enforcement</a> on HCP Terraform to set guardrails for infrastructure operations.</li></ul><h2 id="version-pinning" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="version pinning permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#version-pinning"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Version pinning</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">To prevent provider and module upgrades from introducing unintentional changes to your infrastructure, use version pinning.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Specify provider versions using the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/providers/requirements#requiring-providers">required_providers block</a>. Terraform <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/providers/requirements#version-constraints">version constraints</a> support a range of accepted versions.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Pin modules to a specific major and minor version as shown in the example below to ensure stability. You can use looser restrictions if you are certain that the module does not introduce breaking changes outside of major version updates.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We also recommend that you set a minimum required version of the Terraform binary using the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">required_version</code> in your <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform</code> block. This requires all operators to use a Terraform version that has all of your configuration&#x27;s required features.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_ncbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">terraform</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">  required_providers</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">    aws </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">      source  </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;hashicorp/aws&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight__a84m6 line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">      version </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;5.34.0&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">    }</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  }</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP">
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight__a84m6 line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  required_version </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;&gt;= 1.7&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The above example pins the version of the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">hashicorp/aws</code> provider to version <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">5.34.0</code>, and requires that operators use Terraform <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">1.7</code> or newer.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For modules sourced from a registry, use the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">version</code> parameter in the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">module</code> block to pin the version. For local modules, Terraform ignores the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">version</code> parameter.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 undefined is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_o4bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-function)">module</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-constant)"> &quot;vault_starter&quot;</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)"> {</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  source  </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;hashicorp/vault-starter/aws&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">  version </span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-keyword)">=</span><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-string)"> &quot;1.0.0&quot;</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-comment)">  ##...</span></span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span class="line"><span style="color:var(--code-block-color-token)">}</span></span>
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><h2 id="module-repository-names" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="module repository names permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#module-repository-names"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Module repository names</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The Terraform registry requires that repositories match a naming convention for all modules that you publish to the registry. Module repositories must use this three-part name <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform-&lt;PROVIDER&gt;-&lt;NAME&gt;</code>, where <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">&lt;NAME&gt;</code> reflects the type of infrastructure the module manages and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">&lt;PROVIDER&gt;</code> is the main provider the module uses. The <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">&lt;NAME&gt;</code> segment can contain additional hyphens, for example, <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform-google-vault</code> or <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">terraform-aws-ec2-instance</code>.</p><h2 id="module-structure" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="module structure permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#module-structure"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Module structure</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Terraform modules define self-contained, reusable pieces of infrastructure-as-code.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Use modules to group together logically related resources that you need to provision together. For example:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">A networking module that defines a VPC, along with its subnets, gateway, and security groups.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">An application module defining all resources required for each deployment. This stack could include web servers, databases, storage, and supported networking.</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Review the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/tutorials/modules/pattern-module-creation">module creation recommended pattern documentation</a> and <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/modules/develop/structure">standard module structure</a> for guidance on how to structure your modules.</p><h2 id="local-modules" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="local modules permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#local-modules"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Local modules</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Local modules are sourced from local disk rather than a remote module registry. We recommend publishing your modules to a module registry, such as the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/cloud-docs/registry">HCP Terraform private registry</a>, to easily version, share, and reuse modules across your organization. If you cannot use a module registry, using local modules can simplify maintaining and updating your code.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend that you define child modules in the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">./modules/&lt;module_name&gt;</code> directory.</p><h2 id="repository-structure" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="repository structure permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#repository-structure"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Repository structure</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">How you structure your modules and Terraform configuration in version control significantly impacts versioning and operations. We recommend that you store your actual infrastructure configuration separately from your module code.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Store each module in an individual repository. This lets you independently version each module and makes it easier to publish your modules in the private Terraform registry.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Organize your infrastructure configuration in repositories that group together logically-related resources. For example, a single repository for a web application that requires compute, networking, and database resources . By separating your resources into groups, you limit the number of resources that may be impacted by failures for any operation.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Another approach is to group all modules and infrastructure configuration into a single monolithic repository, or monorepo. For example, a monorepo may define a collection of local modules for each component of the infrastructure stack, and deploy them in the root module.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_s4bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>.</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>├── modules</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── function</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   │   ├── main.tf      # contains aws_iam_role, aws_lambda_function</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   │   ├── outputs.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   │   └── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── queue</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   │   ├── main.tf      # contains aws_sqs_queue</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   │   ├── outputs.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   │   └── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   └── vpc</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│       ├── main.tf      # contains aws_vpc, aws_subnet</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│       ├── outputs.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│       └── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>├── main.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>├── outputs.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>└── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">The advantage of monolithic repositories is having a single source of truth that tracks every infrastructure change. However, monolithic repositories can complicate your CI/CD automation: since any code change triggers a deployment that operates on your entire repository, your workflow must target only the modified directories. You also lose the granular access control, since anyone with repository access can modify any file in it.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If your organization requires a monolithic approach, HCP Terraform and Terraform Enterprise let you scope a workspace to a specific directory in a repository, simplifying your workflows.</p><h2 id="branching-strategy" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="branching strategy permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#branching-strategy"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Branching strategy</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">To collaborate on your Terraform code, we recommend using the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/quickstart/github-flow">GitHub flow</a>. This approach uses short-lived branches to help your team quickly review, test, and merge changes to your code. To make changes to your code, you would:</p><ol class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Create a new branch from your main branch</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Write, commit, and push your changes to the new branch</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Create a pull request</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Review the changes with your team</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Merge the pull request</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Delete the branch</li></ol><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">HCP Terraform and Terraform Enterprise can run <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/cloud-docs/run/ui#speculative-plans-on-pull-requests">speculative plans for pull requests</a>. These speculative plans run automatically when you create or update a pull request, and you can use them to see the effect that your changes will have on your infrastructure before you merge them to your main branch. When you merge your pull request, HCP Terraform will start a new run to apply these changes.</p><h2 id="multiple-environments" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="multiple environments permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#multiple-environments"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Multiple environments</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend that your repository&#x27;s <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">main</code> branch be the source of truth for all environments. For HCP Terraform and Terraform Enterprise users, we recommend that you use separate workspaces for each environment. For larger codebases, we recommend that you split your resources across multiple workspaces to prevent large state files and limit unintended consequences from changes. For example, you could structure your code as follows:</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_ucbakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>.</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>├── compute</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── main.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── outputs.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   └── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>├── database</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── main.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── outputs.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   └── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>└── networking</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>    ├── main.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>    ├── outputs.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>    └── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm">
</span></span></code></pre></div></div><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">In this scenario, you would create three workspaces per environment. For example, your production environment would have a <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">prod-compute</code>, <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">prod-database</code>, and <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">prod-networking</code> workspace. Read more about <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/well-architected-framework/operational-excellence/operational-excellence-workspaces-projects">Terraform workspace and project best practices</a>.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you do not use HCP Terraform or Terraform Enterprise, we recommend that you use modules to encapsulate your configuration, and use a directory for each environment so that each one has a separate state file. The configuration in each of these directories would call the local modules, each with parameters specific to their environment. This also lets you maintain separate variable and backend configurations for each environment.</p><div class="code-block__dOm6M dark__aBMo7 is-standalone__WLWxy"><div class="header__42Fek"></div><div class="body__pLFmP"><pre class="code__J06se" id="_R_v4bakkqcm_" style="max-height:auto"><code><span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>├── modules</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── compute</span>

</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── backend.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   ├── main.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>│   └── variables.tf</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>└── staging</span>
</span><span class="line-of-code__mDztA line-highlight-first__OMDUP line-highlight-last__qC6nm"><span>    ├── backend.tf</span>

</span></span></code></pre></div></div><h2 id="state-sharing" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="state sharing permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#state-sharing"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>State sharing</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Since your state contains sensitive information, avoid sharing full state files when possible.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you use HCP Terraform or Terraform Enterprise and need to reference resources across workspaces, use the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://registry.terraform.io/providers/hashicorp/tfe/latest/docs/data-sources/outputs"><code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">tfe_outputs</code></a> data source.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you do not use HCP Terraform or Terraform Enterprise but still need to reference data about other infrastructure resources, use data sources to query the provider. For example, you can use the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://registry.terraform.io/providers/hashicorp/aws/latest/docs/data-sources/instance"><code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">aws_instance</code> data source</a> to look up an AWS EC2 instance by its ID or tags.</p><h2 id="secrets-management" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="secrets management permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#secrets-management"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Secrets management</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you do not configure remote state storage, the Terraform CLI stores the entire state in plaintext on the local disk. State can include sensitive data, such as passwords and private keys. HCP Terraform and Terraform Enterprise provide state encryption through HashiCorp Vault.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you use HCP Terraform or Terraform Enterprise, we recommend the following:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">When using Terraform Enterprise, define and enforce a Sentinel policy to prevent use of the <code class="mdx-inline-code_inlineCode__p_0sy mdx-inline-code_size-200__YiF_7 hds-typography-code-200">local_exec</code> provisioner or external data sources.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">When using HCP Terraform or Terraform Enterprise, use <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/tutorials/cloud/dynamic-credentials">dynamic provider credentials</a> to avoid using long-lived static credentials.</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you use Terraform Community Edition, we recommend the following:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Configure provider credentials using provider-specific environment variables.</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Access secrets from a secrets management system such as HashiCorp Vault with the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://registry.terraform.io/providers/hashicorp/vault/latest/docs">Terraform Vault provider</a>. Be aware that Terraform will still write these values in plaintext to your state file.</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">If you use a custom CI/CD pipeline, review your CI/CD tool&#x27;s best practices for managing sensitive values. Most tools let you access sensitive values as environment variables. For more information, refer to your CI/CD documentation.</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://docs.github.com/en/actions/security-guides/using-secrets-in-github-actions">Using secrets in GitHub Actions</a></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="https://docs.gitlab.com/ee/ci/pipelines/pipeline_security.html">Gitlab pipeline security</a></li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg"><a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/well-architected-framework/security/security-cicd-vault">Integrate Vault into your CI/CD pipeline</a></li></ul><h2 id="integration-and-unit-testing" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="integration and unit testing permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#integration-and-unit-testing"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Integration and unit testing</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Terraform tests let you validate your modules and catch breaking changes. We recommend that you write tests for your Terraform modules and run them just as you run your tests for your application code, such as pre-merge check in your pull requests or as a prerequisite step in your automated CI/CD pipeline.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Tests differ from validation methods such as variable validation, preconditions, postconditions, and check blocks. These features focus on verifying the infrastructure deployed by your code, while tests validate the behavior and logic of your code itself. For more information, refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/language/tests">Terraform test documentation</a> and the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/tutorials/configuration-language/test">Write Terraform tests tutorial</a>.</p><h2 id="policy" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold mdx-headings_heading__M6qPe g-offset-scroll-margin-top mdx-headings_h2__9ZcX_"><a class="mdx-heading-permalink_root__2oeix g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow mdx-heading-permalink_h2__CA9g8" aria-label="policy permalink" href="/terraform/language/style#policy"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" class="mdx-heading-permalink_icon__Sz01O"><g fill="currentColor"><path d="M11.141 1a3.893 3.893 0 00-2.738 1.093L7.314 3.175A.75.75 0 008.372 4.24l1.077-1.07a2.393 2.393 0 013.384 3.382l-1.881 1.88a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.608-.258.75.75 0 00-1.202.899 3.893 3.893 0 005.87.42l1.886-1.886.01-.009A3.893 3.893 0 0011.14 1z"></path><path d="M7.019 5.365a3.893 3.893 0 00-3.032 1.13L2.102 8.382l-.01.01a3.893 3.893 0 005.505 5.504l1.084-1.084a.75.75 0 00-1.06-1.06l-1.07 1.07a2.393 2.393 0 01-3.384-3.384l1.881-1.88a2.393 2.393 0 013.609.258.75.75 0 101.2-.899A3.893 3.893 0 007.02 5.365z"></path></g></svg></a>Policy</h2><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">Policies are rules that HCP Terraform enforces on Terraform runs. You can use policies to validate that the Terraform plan complies with your organization&#x27;s best practices. For example, you can write policies that:</p><ul class="mdx-lists_listRoot__iVDx9"><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Limit the size of a web instance</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Check for required resource tags</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Block deployments on Fridays</li><li class="mdx-lists_listItem__nkqhg">Enforce security configuration and cost management</li></ul><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">We recommend that you store policies in a separate VCS repository from your Terraform code.</p><p class="text_root__r0DFB hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular mdx-p_p__ZTJEA">For more information, refer to the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/cloud-docs/policy-enforcement">policy enforcement documentation</a>, as well as the <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/tutorials/policy">enforce policy with Sentinel</a> and <a class="inline-link_root__iIql6 g-focus-ring-from-box-shadow hds-typography-body-300 hds-font-weight-regular inline-link_primary__kGJLJ" href="/terraform/tutorials/cloud/drift-and-policy">detect infrastructure drift and enforce policies</a> tutorials.</p><h2 id="next-steps" class="hds-typography-display-500 hds-font-weight-bold Terraform loads this and all files ending with `,mdx(\"inlineCode\",{parentName:\"li\"},`_override.tf`),` last. Use them sparingly and add comments to the original resource definitions, as these overrides make your code harder to reason about. A linter uses static code analysis to compare your Terraform code against a set of rules.
